982 entries, be-test-runner to bff.implementer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **beacon-auditor**: Adversarial completeness auditor for a BEACON epic. Reads the epic's artefact tree (owned specs, stubs, and tasks) in a fresh context and reports where the stated intent — Success criteria, Non-goals, Dependencies, linked ADRs — is not yet represented by an artefact. Use during DESIGN, before building, to confirm the epic is fully decomposed. ([darth-veitcher/comfydv](https://github.com/darth-veitcher/comfydv/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/beacon-auditor.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **beacon-docs-reviewer**: Read-only reviewer for documentation. Reads the changed README.md, docs/, AGENTS.md, --help text and project artefacts (ADRs, epics, specs, plans) in a fresh context. Compares each factual claim with the code, the CLI or API help and the tests, and checks the text against docs/WRITING.md for what `beacon docs check` cannot check. Gives all findings in one pass, wrong facts first, each with the file, the line and a fix. Told "fixes only", it reviews only the diff it is given. Use after the beacon-docs procedure writes a change, before the PR. ([darth-veitcher/comfydv](https://github.com/darth-veitcher/comfydv/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/beacon-docs-reviewer.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **beacon-engineering**: Adversarial engineering lens for a BEACON project. Challenges the technical feasibility of success criteria, calls out missing foundational work, validates that epic/spec sequencing respects actual build dependencies, and flags scope that can't ship independently. Use before building starts to stress-test the plan. Spawned by /beacon.engineering and /beacon.align. ([darth-veitcher/comfydv](https://github.com/darth-veitcher/comfydv/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/beacon-engineering.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **beacon-product**: Strategic PM lens for a BEACON project. Reads the full artefact tree — problem statement, roadmap, epics, active bullets — and reports whether active work is tracking toward the stated goals and quarter vision. Use when in doubt about direction, sequencing, or scope. Spawned by /beacon.product. - **beekeeper**: The BEEKEEPER 🐝 (formerly supervisor) — the gardener's per-feature pipeline warden, one per feature the gardener hands off. Owns the flow from launch to result: extracts the next agent's context, selects and dispatches it, watches the orchard lifecycle, verifies death and timeout, and fires the groundskeeper close in reverse creation order. It choreographs — it never authors the work, never judges it (that is Valve), and never kills (Decision-081). It releases what it created; the gardener releases it. ([kaukea/orchard](https://github.com/kaukea/orchard/blob/HEAD/agents/beekeeper.md))

- **beforeafter**: **Method.** - Lock-in on a tracked phase: phi = 2*pi*integral(2.089 * v_ref) dt, with v_ref = 1 s mean of v_actual. Hann window and linear detrend. - Background = median lock-in at the off-orders 0.6, 0.8, 1.3, 1.6, 1.75, 2.6 and 2.8. - Broadband = HP &gt; 0.7 Hz RMS after a least-squares removal of orders 1, 2, 2.31 and 3. - Data mask: purges ±0.5 s (V_fc dips &gt; 1 V below a 5 s median: 20 before, 18 ([Tangineering/DC-Balancer-Board-Teensy](https://github.com/Tangineering/DC-Balancer-Board-Teensy/blob/HEAD/docs/reviews/noise_fw37_2026-11-05/agents/beforeafter.md) · ★1)

- **bff.analyzer**: Prototype-to-requirements extractor for BFF development. Analyzes Figma or Google Stitch screens and user flows to produce structured user stories, data entities, API requirements, and auth/edge-case documentation. USE FOR: extracting API requirements from Figma or Google Stitch designs, identifying data entities from UI screens, producing user stories from prototype flows, documenting auth and permission requirements from prototype interactions. DO NOT USE FOR: architecture design (use bff.architect), API contract creation (use bff.api-designer), backend implementation. ([DINHDUY/ai-workflow-kit](https://github.com/DINHDUY/ai-workflow-kit/blob/HEAD/workflows/backend-for-frontend/agents/bff.analyzer.md))
- **bff.api-designer**: API contract designer for BFF development. Creates complete OpenAPI 3.0 specifications and mock servers from architecture blueprints. Enables parallel frontend/backend development by providing a working mock immediately. USE FOR: generating OpenAPI specs from ERDs and user stories, designing REST API endpoints and schemas, creating JSON Server mock servers for frontend development, building Postman collections, designing error codes and pagination conventions. DO NOT USE FOR: architecture decisions (use bff.architect), backend implementation (use bff.implementer), prototype analysis. ([DINHDUY/ai-workflow-kit](https://github.com/DINHDUY/ai-workflow-kit/blob/HEAD/workflows/backend-for-frontend/agents/bff.api-designer.md))
- **bff.architect**: Architecture designer for BFF development. Transforms analyzed prototype requirements into a complete architecture blueprint: ERD, tech stack selection, auth strategy, service boundaries, and database schema. USE FOR: designing data models from UI entity analysis, selecting tech stack for BFF projects, creating ERDs from prototype requirements, defining auth architecture, planning service layer structure. DO NOT USE FOR: prototype analysis (use bff.analyzer), API contract design (use bff.api-designer), backend implementation. ([DINHDUY/ai-workflow-kit](https://github.com/DINHDUY/ai-workflow-kit/blob/HEAD/workflows/backend-for-frontend/agents/bff.architect.md))
- **bff.implementer**: Backend implementation agent for BFF development. Builds a complete TypeScript backend from an OpenAPI spec and ERD: TypeScript project setup, Prisma schema generation, Express routes/controllers/services, JWT auth middleware, Zod validation, structured logging, and error handling. USE FOR: scaffolding a TypeScript Express backend from an API spec, generating Prisma schema from an ERD, implementing JWT authentication with Passport.js, building REST API controllers and services, wiring Zod validation middleware. DO NOT USE FOR: API contract design (use bff.api-designer), architecture planning… ([DINHDUY/ai-workflow-kit](https://github.com/DINHDUY/ai-workflow-kit/blob/HEAD/workflows/backend-for-frontend/agents/bff.implementer.md))
